Here is a new customer's task: Develop a Checkers (Draughts) game. Use an 8x8 board, alternate turns between two players, and apply standard capture and kinging rules. Prompt for moves in notation (e.g., from-to positions) and update the board state accordingly..
To complete the task, You must write a response that appropriately solves the requested instruction based on your expertise and customer's needs.]

Below is a clean, fully functional Python/Tkinter implementation of an 8x8 Checkers (Draughts) game with:

- alternating turns between two players
- standard diagonal movement
- mandatory captures
- kinging when a piece reaches the far end
- move input in notation like `b6-a5`
- board updates and move validation
- a simple graphical GUI for play

## Core components

### Main classes / functions
- **`CheckersGame`**: main game controller, holds board state, validates and applies moves.
- **`CheckersGUI`**: Tkinter GUI, renders the board and handles user interaction.
- **`parse_move_notation()`**: converts move text like `b6-a5` into board coordinates.
- **`position_to_coords()` / `coords_to_position()`**: converts between human-readable positions and grid indices.
- **`get_valid_moves_for_piece()`**: calculates legal moves and captures for a selected piece.
- **`apply_move()`**: performs the move, capture removal, promotion to king, and turn switching.
- **`has_any_valid_move()` / `get_winner()`**: detect end-game conditions.

---

# main.py

```python
'''
main entry point for the checkers game application
'''

from gui import CheckersGUI


def main():
    """Start the Checkers GUI application."""
    app = CheckersGUI()
    app.run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# game.py

```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''

from dataclasses import dataclass


BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""


def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"


def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col


def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst


class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None

    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]

        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2

        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1

        return board

    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None

    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None

    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)

    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]

    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}

    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE

    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.

        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}

        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}

        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)

        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))

        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}

    def has_any_capture(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                piece = self.board[r][c]
                if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                    moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                    if moves["captures"]:
                        return True
        return False

    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")

        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")

        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")

        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.has_any_capture(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")

        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY

        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY

        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING

        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1

        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2

        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")

    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner

    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

# gui.py

```python
'''
tkinter gui for the checkers application
'''

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox

from game import (
    CheckersGame,
    coords_to_position,
    parse_move_notation,
    PLAYER_1,
    PLAYER_2,
    PLAYER_1_KING,
    PLAYER_2_KING,
)


class CheckersGUI:
    """Tkinter-based graphical interface for Checkers."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.root.title("Checkers (Draughts)")
        self.root.resizable(False, False)

        self.game = CheckersGame()
        self.selected_square = None

        self.board_frame = tk.Frame(self.root, padx=10, pady=10)
        self.board_frame.grid(row=0, column=0, columnspan=2)

        self.info_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.info_var.set("Red to move. Enter moves like b6-a5.")

        self.status_label = tk.Label(self.root, textvariable=self.info_var, font=("Arial", 12))
        self.status_label.grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, pady=(0, 8))

        self.move_entry = tk.Entry(self.root, width=20, font=("Arial", 12))
        self.move_entry.grid(row=2, column=0, padx=10, pady=10, sticky="ew")
        self.move_entry.bind("<Return>", self.on_submit_move)

        self.submit_button = tk.Button(self.root, text="Move", command=self.on_submit_move)
        self.submit_button.grid(row=2, column=1, padx=10, pady=10, sticky="ew")

        self.reset_button = tk.Button(self.root, text="Reset", command=self.on_reset)
        self.reset_button.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, padx=10, pady=(0, 10), sticky="ew")

        self.cells = [[None for _ in range(8)] for _ in range(8)]
        self._build_board()
        self.refresh_board()

    def _build_board(self):
        """Create the clickable board grid."""
        for r in range(8):
            for c in range(8):
                btn = tk.Button(
                    self.board_frame,
                    width=4,
                    height=2,
                    font=("Arial", 18, "bold"),
                    command=lambda row=r, col=c: self.on_square_click(row, col),
                )
                btn.grid(row=r, column=c, padx=0, pady=0)
                self.cells[r][c] = btn

    def square_color(self, row, col):
        """Return checkerboard square color."""
        return "#DDB88C" if (row + col) % 2 == 0 else "#A66A3F"

    def piece_symbol(self, piece):
        """Map piece code to display symbol."""
        return {
            ".": "",
            PLAYER_1: "●",
            PLAYER_2: "○",
            PLAYER_1_KING: "♔",
            PLAYER_2_KING: "♚",
        }.get(piece, "")

    def refresh_board(self):
        """Refresh the GUI from the current game state."""
        for r in range(8):
            for c in range(8):
                btn = self.cells[r][c]
                piece = self.game.board[r][c]
                btn.configure(
                    text=self.piece_symbol(piece),
                    bg=self.square_color(r, c),
                    fg="red" if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING) else "black",
                    relief=tk.SUNKEN if self.selected_square == (r, c) else tk.RAISED,
                )

        if self.game.game_over:
            winner = "Red" if self.game.winner == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
            self.info_var.set(f"Game over. {winner} wins.")
        else:
            self.info_var.set(f"{self.game.current_player_name()} to move. Enter moves like b6-a5.")

    def on_square_click(self, row, col):
        """Handle clicks for convenience selection and move entry assistance."""
        if self.game.game_over:
            return

        if self.selected_square is None:
            piece = self.game.board[row][col]
            if self.game.piece_owner(piece) == self.game.current_player:
                self.selected_square = (row, col)
                self.refresh_board()
        else:
            src_row, src_col = self.selected_square
            move_text = f"{coords_to_position(src_row, src_col)}-{coords_to_position(row, col)}"
            self.move_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.move_entry.insert(0, move_text)
            self.selected_square = None
            self.refresh_board()

    def on_submit_move(self, event=None):
        """Apply the move from the input box."""
        move_text = self.move_entry.get().strip()
        if not move_text:
            return

        try:
            (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
        except ValueError as exc:
            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Move", str(exc))
            return

        result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
        if not result.success:
            messagebox.showerror("Illegal Move", result.message)
            return

        self.selected_square = None
        self.move_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
        self.refresh_board()

        if self.game.game_over:
            winner = "Red" if self.game.winner == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
            messagebox.showinfo("Game Over", f"{winner} wins!")

    def on_reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the initial state."""
        self.game.reset()
        self.selected_square = None
        self.move_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
        self.refresh_board()

    def run(self):
        """Run the Tkinter event loop."""
        self.root.mainloop()
```

## How to run

1. Save the files as:
   - `main.py`
   - `game.py`
   - `gui.py`

2. Run:

```bash
python main.py
```

## Notes
- Moves are entered using notation like `b6-a5`.
- Captures are enforced when available.
- Kings move in both directions after promotion.
- The GUI also supports clicking a piece and then a destination square to auto-fill the move notation.

If you want, I can also provide:
- a **Pygame version** with nicer graphics,
- **multi-jump capture chains**,
- or **computer opponent / AI**.

- a simple graphical GUI for play

# game.py

```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''

from dataclasses import dataclass


BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""


def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"


def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col


def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst


class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None

    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]

        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2

        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1

        return board

    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None

    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None

    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)

    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]

    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}

    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE

    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.

        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}

        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}

        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)

        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))

        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}

    def has_any_capture(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                piece = self.board[r][c]
                if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                    moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                    if moves["captures"]:
                        return True
        return False

    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")

        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")

        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")

        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.has_any_capture(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")

        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY

        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY

        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING

        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1

        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2

        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")

    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner

    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

# gui.py

```python
'''
tkinter gui for the checkers application
'''

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox

from game import (
    CheckersGame,
    coords_to_position,
    parse_move_notation,
    PLAYER_1,
    PLAYER_2,
    PLAYER_1_KING,
    PLAYER_2_KING,
)


class CheckersGUI:
    """Tkinter-based graphical interface for Checkers."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.root.title("Checkers (Draughts)")
        self.root.resizable(False, False)

        self.game = CheckersGame()
        self.selected_square = None

        self.board_frame = tk.Frame(self.root, padx=10, pady=10)
        self.board_frame.grid(row=0, column=0, columnspan=2)

        self.info_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.info_var.set("Red to move. Enter moves like b6-a5.")

        self.status_label = tk.Label(self.root, textvariable=self.info_var, font=("Arial", 12))
        self.status_label.grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, pady=(0, 8))

        self.move_entry = tk.Entry(self.root, width=20, font=("Arial", 12))
        self.move_entry.grid(row=2, column=0, padx=10, pady=10, sticky="ew")
        self.move_entry.bind("<Return>", self.on_submit_move)

        self.submit_button = tk.Button(self.root, text="Move", command=self.on_submit_move)
        self.submit_button.grid(row=2, column=1, padx=10, pady=10, sticky="ew")

        self.reset_button = tk.Button(self.root, text="Reset", command=self.on_reset)
        self.reset_button.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, padx=10, pady=(0, 10), sticky="ew")

        self.cells = [[None for _ in range(8)] for _ in range(8)]
        self._build_board()
        self.refresh_board()

    def _build_board(self):
        """Create the clickable board grid."""
        for r in range(8):
            for c in range(8):
                btn = tk.Button(
                    self.board_frame,
                    width=4,
                    height=2,
                    font=("Arial", 18, "bold"),
                    command=lambda row=r, col=c: self.on_square_click(row, col),
                )
                btn.grid(row=r, column=c, padx=0, pady=0)
                self.cells[r][c] = btn

    def square_color(self, row, col):
        """Return checkerboard square color."""
        return "#DDB88C" if (row + col) % 2 == 0 else "#A66A3F"

    def piece_symbol(self, piece):
        """Map piece code to display symbol."""
        return {
            ".": "",
            PLAYER_1: "●",
            PLAYER_2: "○",
            PLAYER_1_KING: "♔",
            PLAYER_2_KING: "♚",
        }.get(piece, "")

    def refresh_board(self):
        """Refresh the GUI from the current game state."""
        for r in range(8):
            for c in range(8):
                btn = self.cells[r][c]
                piece = self.game.board[r][c]
                btn.configure(
                    text=self.piece_symbol(piece),
                    bg=self.square_color(r, c),
                    fg="red" if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING) else "black",
                    relief=tk.SUNKEN if self.selected_square == (r, c) else tk.RAISED,
                )

        if self.game.game_over:
            winner = "Red" if self.game.winner == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
            self.info_var.set(f"Game over. {winner} wins.")
        else:
            self.info_var.set(f"{self.game.current_player_name()} to move. Enter moves like b6-a5.")

    def on_square_click(self, row, col):
        """Handle clicks for convenience selection and move entry assistance."""
        if self.game.game_over:
            return

        if self.selected_square is None:
            piece = self.game.board[row][col]
            if self.game.piece_owner(piece) == self.game.current_player:
                self.selected_square = (row, col)
                self.refresh_board()
        else:
            src_row, src_col = self.selected_square
            move_text = f"{coords_to_position(src_row, src_col)}-{coords_to_position(row, col)}"
            self.move_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.move_entry.insert(0, move_text)
            self.selected_square = None
            self.refresh_board()

    def on_submit_move(self, event=None):
        """Apply the move from the input box."""
        move_text = self.move_entry.get().strip()
        if not move_text:
            return

        try:
            (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
        except ValueError as exc:
            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Move", str(exc))
            return

        result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
        if not result.success:
            messagebox.showerror("Illegal Move", result.message)
            return

        self.selected_square = None
        self.move_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
        self.refresh_board()

        if self.game.game_over:
            winner = "Red" if self.game.winner == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
            messagebox.showinfo("Game Over", f"{winner} wins!")

    def on_reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the initial state."""
        self.game.reset()
        self.selected_square = None
        self.move_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
        self.refresh_board()

    def run(self):
        """Run the Tkinter event loop."""
        self.root.mainloop()
```

## How to run

1. Save the files as:
   - `main.py`
   - `game.py`
   - `gui.py`

2. Run:

```bash
python main.py
```

## Notes
- Moves are entered using notation like `b6-a5`.
- Captures are enforced when available.
- Kings move in both directions after promotion.
- The GUI also supports clicking a piece and then a destination square to auto-fill the move notation.

If you want, I can also provide:
- a **Pygame version** with nicer graphics,
- **multi-jump capture chains**,
- or **computer opponent / AI**.

```
'''
```

```
-# None
+'''
+checkers game logic and rules engine
+'''
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+BOARD_SIZE = 8
+EMPTY = "."
+PLAYER_1 = "r"
+PLAYER_2 = "b"
+PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
+PLAYER_2_KING = "B"
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class MoveResult:
+    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
+    success: bool
+    message: str = ""
+def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
+    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
+    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"
+def position_to_coords(pos: str):
+    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
+    pos = pos.strip().lower()
+    if len(pos) < 2:
+        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
+    col_char = pos[0]
+    row_part = pos[1:]
+    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
+        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
+    if not row_part.isdigit():
+        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
+    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
+    row_num = int(row_part)
+    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
+        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
+    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
+    return row, col
+def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
+    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
+    if "-" not in move_text:
+        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
+    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
+    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
+    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
+    return src, dst
+class CheckersGame:
+    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules."""
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
+        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
+        self.game_over = False
+        self.winner = None
+    def _create_initial_board(self):
+        """Create the standard starting board."""
+        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
+        for row in range(3):
+            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
+                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
+        for row in range(5, 8):
+            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
+                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
+        return board
+    def reset(self):
+        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
+        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
+        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
+        self.game_over = False
+        self.winner = None
+    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
+        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
+        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
+            return PLAYER_1
+        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
+            return PLAYER_2
+        return None
+    def is_king(self, piece: str):
+        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
+        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)
+    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
+        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
+        if piece == PLAYER_1:
+            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
+        if piece == PLAYER_2:
+            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
+        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]
+    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
+        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
+        if player == PLAYER_1:
+            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
+        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}
+    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
+        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
+        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE
+    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
+        """
+        Return valid moves for a piece.
+        Result format:
+        {
+            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
+            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
+        }
+        """
+        piece = self.board[row][col]
+        if piece == EMPTY:
+            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
+        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
+        if owner != self.current_player:
+            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
+        simple_moves = []
+        captures = []
+        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
+        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
+        for dr, dc in dirs:
+            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
+            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
+                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
+                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
+                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
+                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
+                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
+                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
+        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}
+    def has_any_capture(self, player: str):
+        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
+        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+                piece = self.board[r][c]
+                if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
+                    moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
+                    if moves["captures"]:
+                        return True
+        return False
+    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
+        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
+        current = self.current_player
+        self.current_player = player
+        try:
+            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+                    piece = self.board[r][c]
+                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
+                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
+                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
+                            return True
+            return False
+        finally:
+            self.current_player = current
+    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
+        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
+        if self.game_over:
+            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
+        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
+        if piece == EMPTY:
+            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
+        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
+            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
+        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
+        capture_move = None
+        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
+            if self.has_any_capture(self.current_player):
+                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
+        else:
+            for cap in legal["captures"]:
+                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
+                    capture_move = cap
+                    break
+            if capture_move is None:
+                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
+        if capture_move:
+            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
+            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
+        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
+        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
+        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
+            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
+        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
+            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
+        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
+        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
+            self.game_over = True
+            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
+        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")
+    def board_text(self):
+        """Return a text representation of the board."""
+        lines = []
+        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
+        lines.append(header)
+        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
+            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
+            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
+                line.append(self.board[r][c])
+            lines.append(" ".join(line))
+        return "\n".join(lines)
+    def get_winner(self):
+        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
+        return self.winner
+    def current_player_name(self):
+        """Human-readable current player name."""
+        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass
BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""
def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"
def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col
def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst
class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board
    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None
    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)
    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]
    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}
    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE
    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}
    def has_any_capture(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                piece = self.board[r][c]
                if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                    moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                    if moves["captures"]:
                        return True
        return False
    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.has_any_capture(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")
    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)
    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner
    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

```python
"""
Command-line interface for the checkers game.
"""
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation

class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple text-based interface for playing checkers."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()

    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())
            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                print(f"Game over! Winner: {'Red' if winner == 'r' else 'Black'}")
                break

            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            move_text = input("Enter move (e.g., b6-a5) or 'quit': ").strip()
            if move_text.lower() == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break

            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
```

### Additional important review notes
- `main.py` is fine once `gui.py` exists.
- `game.py` is mostly solid, but I recommend one follow-up fix:
  - **Capture chaining is not implemented.** In standard checkers, after a capture, if the same piece can capture again, the player must continue. Your current code switches turns immediately after one capture. If you want strict “standard capture rules,” this should be added.
- Also, `has_any_valid_move()` temporarily mutates `current_player`; it works, but it is fragile. A cleaner approach is to make `get_valid_moves_for_piece()` accept a `player` argument instead of relying on global state.

### Bottom line
Fix `gui.py` first, because the app is currently not runnable.

game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass
BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""
def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"
def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col
def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst
class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board
    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None
    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)
    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]
    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}
    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE
    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}
    def has_any_capture(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                piece = self.board[r][c]
                if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                    moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                    if moves["captures"]:
                        return True
        return False
    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.has_any_capture(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")
    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)
    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner
    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

```python
python main.py
```

"
Comments on Codes:
"Highest-priority comment: **The project is not directly operable because `gui.py` is missing/invalid, and `main.py` depends on `CheckersGUI` that is never defined or imported.** Right now `python main.py` will fail immediately, so the app cannot satisfy the “prompt for moves and update the board state” requirement.

### Why this is critical
- `main.py` imports `from gui import CheckersGUI`
- But the provided `gui.py` content is just:
  ```python
  python main.py
  ```
  which is not valid Python code.
- Therefore, the entry point crashes before any gameplay begins.
- Also, the customer asked for a move-prompting application; the current code only contains the game engine, not a usable interface.

### How to fix
1. **Implement a real `gui.py` module** that defines `CheckersGUI`.
2. Since the task is to “prompt for moves in notation (e.g., from-to positions)”, a **CLI interface** is acceptable and simpler than a graphical GUI unless GUI is explicitly required.
3. The interface should:
   - print the board,
   - show whose turn it is,
   - accept input like `b6-a5`,
   - call `CheckersGame.apply_move(...)`,
   - display success/error messages,
   - stop when the game ends,
   - allow reset/restart if desired.

### Suggested minimal `gui.py` replacement
```python
"""
Command-line interface for the checkers game.
"""
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation

class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple text-based interface for playing checkers."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()

    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())
            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                print(f"Game over! Winner: {'Red' if winner == 'r' else 'Black'}")
                break

            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            move_text = input("Enter move (e.g., b6-a5) or 'quit': ").strip()
            if move_text.lower() == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break

            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
```

### Additional important review notes
- `main.py` is fine once `gui.py` exists.
- `game.py` is mostly solid, but I recommend one follow-up fix:
  - **Capture chaining is not implemented.** In standard checkers, after a capture, if the same piece can capture again, the player must continue. Your current code switches turns immediately after one capture. If you want strict “standard capture rules,” this should be added.
- Also, `has_any_valid_move()` temporarily mutates `current_player`; it works, but it is fragile. A cleaner approach is to make `get_valid_moves_for_piece()` accept a `player` argument instead of relying on global state.

### Bottom line
Fix `gui.py` first, because the app is currently not runnable."

game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass

BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""


def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"


def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col


def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    move_text = move_text.strip()
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst


class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None

    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board

    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None

    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None

    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)

    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]

    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}

    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE

    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}

        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)

        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}

    def _has_any_capture_for_player(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return []
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner is None:
            return []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        caps = []
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
            if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return caps

    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")

        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):
            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")

        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")

        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None

        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")

        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY

        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY

        became_king = False
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
            became_king = True
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
            became_king = True

        # Handle multi-capture opportunity for the same piece.
        if capture_move:
            continued_captures = self._available_captures_from(dst_row, dst_col)
            if continued_captures and not became_king:
                # Same player must continue with the same piece if another capture exists.
                return MoveResult(True, "Capture applied successfully. Another capture is available from the same piece.")
            # If piece just crowned, standard play ends the turn after promotion.
            # If further captures are allowed in your rule set after crowning, that can be extended here.

        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1

        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2

        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")

    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner

    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

gui.py
```python
'''
command-line interface for the checkers game application
'''
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation, PLAYER_1, PLAYER_2


class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple command-line interface for playing checkers."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()

    def _player_name(self, symbol: str) -> str:
        """Convert player symbol to display name."""
        return "Red" if symbol == PLAYER_1 else "Black"

    def _print_help(self):
        """Print game instructions."""
        print("\nCommands:")
        print("  move like: b6-a5")
        print("  reset     : restart the game")
        print("  quit      : exit the game")
        print("")

    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        print("Checkers (Draughts)")
        print("Use move notation like b6-a5")
        self._print_help()

        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())
            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                if winner:
                    print(f"\nGame over! Winner: {self._player_name(winner)}")
                else:
                    print("\nGame over!")
                break

            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            move_text = input("Enter move (or 'help', 'reset', 'quit'): ").strip()

            if not move_text:
                print("Please enter a move.")
                continue

            lowered = move_text.lower()
            if lowered == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break
            if lowered == "help":
                self._print_help()
                continue
            if lowered == "reset":
                self.game.reset()
                print("Game reset.")
                continue

            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
```

**[Seminar Conclusion]**:

```python
'''
main entry point for the checkers game application
'''
from gui import CheckersGUI

def main():
    """Start the Checkers application."""
    app = CheckersGUI()
    app.run()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```python
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
```
