game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass
BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""
def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"
def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col
def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    move_text = move_text.strip()
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst
class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules, including forced captures and multi-capture continuation."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board
    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None
    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)
    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]
    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}
    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE
    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}
    def _has_any_capture_for_player(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return []
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner is None:
            return []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        caps = []
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
            if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return caps
    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):
            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")
        if self.forced_capture_piece is not None and (src_row, src_col) != self.forced_capture_piece:
            return MoveResult(False, "You must continue capturing with the same piece.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture chain is active and you must keep capturing.")
            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        became_king = False
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
            became_king = True
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
            became_king = True
        if capture_move:
            continued_captures = self._available_captures_from(dst_row, dst_col)
            if continued_captures and not became_king:
                self.forced_capture_piece = (dst_row, dst_col)
                return MoveResult(True, "Capture applied successfully. Another capture is required from the same piece.")
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")
    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)
    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner
    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

gui.py
```python
'''
command-line interface for the checkers game application
'''
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation, PLAYER_1, PLAYER_2
class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple command-line interface for playing checkers."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()
    def _player_name(self, symbol: str) -> str:
        """Convert player symbol to display name."""
        return "Red" if symbol == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
    def _print_help(self):
        """Print game instructions."""
        print("\nCommands:")
        print("  move like: b6-a5")
        print("  reset     : restart the game")
        print("  quit      : exit the game")
        print("")
    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        print("Checkers (Draughts)")
        print("Use move notation like b6-a5")
        self._print_help()
        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())
            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                if winner:
                    print(f"\nGame over! Winner: {self._player_name(winner)}")
                else:
                    print("\nGame over!")
                break
            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            if self.game.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                forced_row, forced_col = self.game.forced_capture_piece
                from game import coords_to_position
                print(f"Forced capture continuation from {coords_to_position(forced_row, forced_col)}")
            move_text = input("Enter move (or 'help', 'reset', 'quit'): ").strip()
            if not move_text:
                print("Please enter a move.")
                continue
            lowered = move_text.lower()
            if lowered == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break
            if lowered == "help":
                self._print_help()
                continue
            if lowered == "reset":
                self.game.reset()
                print("Game reset.")
                continue
            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
```

Error Summary of Test Reports:
"The immediate bug causing the test failure is:

### 1) `input()` is used in a non-interactive test environment
- In `gui.py`, the game loop calls:
  ```python
  move_text = input("Enter move (or 'help', 'reset', 'quit'): ").strip()
  ```
- Your test run appears to be non-interactive, so `input()` raises:
  ```python
  EOFError: EOF when reading a line
  ```
- This is not a rule-engine bug; it is a **runtime I/O bug** caused by assuming standard input is available.

---

## Summary of the problem
### Root cause
The application starts an interactive command-line loop unconditionally in `main.py`:
```python
app = CheckersGUI()
app.run()
```
and `run()` always expects user input via `input()`.

### Effect
When executed in an environment without stdin (such as automated tests), the program crashes immediately before any gameplay occurs.

---

## Other potential issues in the codebase
These did **not** cause the reported traceback, but are worth noting:

### 2) GUI naming mismatch
- `CheckersGUI` is actually a CLI, not a GUI.
- This is only a naming/design issue, not a crash cause.

### 3) No graceful handling of EOF / interrupted input
- If input ends unexpectedly, the program should catch `EOFError` and exit cleanly instead of crashing.

---

## In short
The bug is:
- **Unprotected use of `input()` in `gui.py`**
- Triggered by **running the program in a non-interactive environment**
- Resulting in **`EOFError`**

If you want, I can also propose a minimal code fix to make the program pass automated tests cleanly."

game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass

BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""

def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"

def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col

def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    move_text = move_text.strip()
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst

class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules, including forced captures and multi-capture continuation."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None

    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board

    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None

    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None

    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)

    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]

    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}

    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE

    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}

    def _has_any_capture_for_player(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return []
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner is None:
            return []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        caps = []
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
            if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return caps

    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):
            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")
        if self.forced_capture_piece is not None and (src_row, src_col) != self.forced_capture_piece:
            return MoveResult(False, "You must continue capturing with the same piece.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture chain is active and you must keep capturing.")
            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        became_king = False
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
            became_king = True
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
            became_king = True
        if capture_move:
            continued_captures = self._available_captures_from(dst_row, dst_col)
            if continued_captures and not became_king:
                self.forced_capture_piece = (dst_row, dst_col)
                return MoveResult(True, "Capture applied successfully. Another capture is required from the same piece.")
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")

    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner

    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

gui.py
```python
'''
command-line interface for the checkers game application
'''
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation, PLAYER_1, PLAYER_2, coords_to_position

class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple command-line interface for playing checkers."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()

    def _player_name(self, symbol: str) -> str:
        """Convert player symbol to display name."""
        return "Red" if symbol == PLAYER_1 else "Black"

    def _print_help(self):
        """Print game instructions."""
        print("\nCommands:")
        print("  move like: b6-a5")
        print("  reset     : restart the game")
        print("  quit      : exit the game")
        print("")

    def _safe_input(self, prompt: str):
        """Read input safely and return None on EOF so tests/non-interactive runs do not crash."""
        try:
            return input(prompt)
        except EOFError:
            return None
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            return None

    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        print("Checkers (Draughts)")
        print("Use move notation like b6-a5")
        self._print_help()

        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())

            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                if winner:
                    print(f"\nGame over! Winner: {self._player_name(winner)}")
                else:
                    print("\nGame over!")
                break

            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            if self.game.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                forced_row, forced_col = self.game.forced_capture_piece
                print(f"Forced capture continuation from {coords_to_position(forced_row, forced_col)}")

            move_raw = self._safe_input("Enter move (or 'help', 'reset', 'quit'): ")
            if move_raw is None:
                print("\nNo input available. Exiting cleanly.")
                break

            move_text = move_raw.strip()
            if not move_text:
                print("Please enter a move.")
                continue

            lowered = move_text.lower()
            if lowered == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break
            if lowered == "help":
                self._print_help()
                continue
            if lowered == "reset":
                self.game.reset()
                print("Game reset.")
                continue

            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
```

    app.run()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass

BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""

def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"

def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col

def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    move_text = move_text.strip()
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst

class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules, including forced captures and multi-capture continuation."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None

    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board

    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None

    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None

    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)

    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]

    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}

    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE

    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}

    def _has_any_capture_for_player(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current

    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return []
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner is None:
            return []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        caps = []
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
            if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return caps

    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):
            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")
        if self.forced_capture_piece is not None and (src_row, src_col) != self.forced_capture_piece:
            return MoveResult(False, "You must continue capturing with the same piece.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture chain is active and you must keep capturing.")
            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        became_king = False
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
            became_king = True
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
            became_king = True
        if capture_move:
            continued_captures = self._available_captures_from(dst_row, dst_col)
            if continued_captures and not became_king:
                self.forced_capture_piece = (dst_row, dst_col)
                return MoveResult(True, "Capture applied successfully. Another capture is required from the same piece.")
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")

    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner

    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"

Another capture is required from the same piece.")\n        self.forced_capture_piece = None\n        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1\n        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):\n            self.game_over = True\n            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2\n        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")\n    def board_text(self):\n        """Return a text representation of the board."""\n        lines = []\n        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))\n        lines.append(header)\n        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):\n            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r\n            line = [f"{row_num}  "]\n            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):\n                line.append(self.board[r][c])\n            lines.append(" ".join(line))\n        return "\\n".join(lines)\n    def get_winner(self):\n        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""\n        return self.winner\n    def current_player_name(self):\n        """Human-readable current player name."""\n        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"\n\n\ngui.py\npython\n\'\'\'\ncommand-line interface for the checkers game application\n\'\'\'\nfrom game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation, PLAYER_1, PLAYER_2, coords_to_position\nclass CheckersGUI:\n    """Simple command-line interface for playing checkers."""\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.game = CheckersGame()\n    def _player_name(self, symbol: str) -&gt; str:\n        """Convert player symbol to display name."""\n        return "Red" if symbol == PLAYER_1 else "Black"\n    def _print_help(self):\n        """Print game instructions."""\n        print("\\nCommands:")\n        print("  move like: b6-a5")\n        print("  reset     : restart the game")\n        print("  quit      : exit the game")\n        print("")\n    def _safe_input(self, prompt: str):\n        """Read input safely and return None on EOF so tests/non-interactive runs do not crash."""\n        try:\n            return input(prompt)\n        except EOFError:\n            return None\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            return None\n    def run(self):\n        """Run the interactive game loop."""\n        print("Checkers (Draughts)")\n        print("Use move notation like b6-a5")\n        self._print_help()\n        while True:\n            print(self.game.board_text())\n            if self.game.game_over:\n                winner = self.game.get_winner()\n                if winner:\n                    print(f"\\nGame over! Winner: {self._player_name(winner)}")\n                else:\n                    print("\\nGame over!")\n                break\n            print(f"\\n{self.game.current_player_name()}\'s turn")\n            if self.game.forced_capture_piece is not None:\n                forced_row, forced_col = self.game.forced_capture_piece\n                print(f"Forced capture continuation from {coords_to_position(forced_row, forced_col)}")\n            move_raw = self._safe_input("Enter move (or \'help\', \'reset\', \'quit\'): ")\n            if move_raw is None:\n                print("\\nNo input available. Exiting cleanly.")\n                break\n            move_text = move_raw.strip()\n            if not move_text:\n                print("Please enter a move.")\n                continue\n            lowered = move_text.lower()\n            if lowered == "quit":\n                print("Goodbye.")\n                break\n            if lowered == "help":\n                self._print_help()\n                continue\n            if lowered == "reset":\n                self.game.reset()\n                print("Game reset.")\n                continue\n            try:\n                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)\n                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)\n                print(result.message)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f"Error: {e}")\n\n\n'} |

game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass
BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""
def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"
def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col
def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    move_text = move_text.strip()
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst
class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules, including forced captures and multi-capture continuation."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board
    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None
    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)
    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]
    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}
    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE
    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}
    def _has_any_capture_for_player(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return []
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner is None:
            return []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        caps = []
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
            if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return caps
    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):
            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")
        if self.forced_capture_piece is not None and (src_row, src_col) != self.forced_capture_piece:
            return MoveResult(False, "You must continue capturing with the same piece.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture chain is active and you must keep capturing.")
            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        became_king = False
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
            became_king = True
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
            became_king = True
        if capture_move:
            continued_captures = self._available_captures_from(dst_row, dst_col)
            if continued_captures and not became_king:
                self.forced_capture_piece = (dst_row, dst_col)
                return MoveResult(True, "Capture applied successfully. Another capture is required from the same piece.")
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")
    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)
    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner
    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```

gui.py
```python
'''
command-line interface for the checkers game application
'''
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation, PLAYER_1, PLAYER_2, coords_to_position
class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple command-line interface for playing checkers."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()
    def _player_name(self, symbol: str) -> str:
        """Convert player symbol to display name."""
        return "Red" if symbol == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
    def _print_help(self):
        """Print game instructions."""
        print("\nCommands:")
        print("  move like: b6-a5")
        print("  reset     : restart the game")
        print("  quit      : exit the game")
        print("")
    def _safe_input(self, prompt: str):
        """Read input safely and return None on EOF so tests/non-interactive runs do not crash."""
        try:
            return input(prompt)
        except EOFError:
            return None
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            return None
    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        print("Checkers (Draughts)")
        print("Use move notation like b6-a5")
        self._print_help()
        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())
            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                if winner:
                    print(f"\nGame over! Winner: {self._player_name(winner)}")
                else:
                    print("\nGame over!")
                break
            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            if self.game.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                forced_row, forced_col = self.game.forced_capture_piece
                print(f"Forced capture continuation from {coords_to_position(forced_row, forced_col)}")
            move_raw = self._safe_input("Enter move (or 'help', 'reset', 'quit'): ")
            if move_raw is None:
                print("\nNo input available. Exiting cleanly.")
                break
            move_text = move_raw.strip()
            if not move_text:
                print("Please enter a move.")
                continue
            lowered = move_text.lower()
            if lowered == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break
            if lowered == "help":
                self._print_help()
                continue
            if lowered == "reset":
                self.game.reset()
                print("Game reset.")
                continue
            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
```

"""Check whether a piece is a king."""\n        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)\n    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):\n        """Get movement directions for a piece."""\n        if piece == PLAYER_1:\n            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]\n        if piece == PLAYER_2:\n            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]\n        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]\n    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):\n        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""\n        if player == PLAYER_1:\n            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}\n        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}\n    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):\n        """Check if a position is inside the board."""\n        return 0 &lt;= row &lt; BOARD_SIZE and 0 &lt;= col &lt; current = self.current_player\n        self.current_player = player\n        try:\n            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):\n                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):\n                    piece =                   if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:\n                            return True\n            return False\n        finally:\n            self.current_player = current\n    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):\n        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""\n        piece = self.board[row][col]\n        if piece c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):\n                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:\n                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))\n        return caps\n    def Please reset to play again.")\n        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):\n            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")\n        if self.forced_capture_piece is not None and (src_row, src_col) != self.forced_capture_piece:\n            return MoveResult(False, "You must continue capturing with the same piece.")\n        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]\n        if piece == EMPTY:\n            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")\n        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:\n            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece\'s turn.")\n        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:\n            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")\n        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)\n        capture_move = None\n        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:\n            if self.forced_capture_piece is not None:\n                return MoveResult(False, "A capture chain is active and you must keep capturing.")\n            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):\n                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")\n        else:\n            for cap in legal["captures"]:\n                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):\n                    capture_move = cap\n                    break\n            if capture_move is None:\n                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")\n        if capture_move:\n            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]\n            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY\n        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece\n        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY\n        became_king = False\n        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:\n            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING\n            became_king = True\n        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - game.py
```python
'''
checkers game logic and rules engine
'''
from dataclasses import dataclass
BOARD_SIZE = 8
EMPTY = "."
PLAYER_1 = "r"
PLAYER_2 = "b"
PLAYER_1_KING = "R"
PLAYER_2_KING = "B"
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MoveResult:
    """Represents the outcome of attempting a move."""
    success: bool
    message: str = ""
def coords_to_position(row: int, col: int) -> str:
    """Convert board coordinates to human-readable notation like b6."""
    return f"{chr(ord('a') + col)}{BOARD_SIZE - row}"
def position_to_coords(pos: str):
    """Convert human-readable notation like b6 to board coordinates."""
    pos = pos.strip().lower()
    if len(pos) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid position format.")
    col_char = pos[0]
    row_part = pos[1:]
    if not ('a' <= col_char <= 'h'):
        raise ValueError("Column must be between a and h.")
    if not row_part.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Row must be numeric.")
    col = ord(col_char) - ord('a')
    row_num = int(row_part)
    if not (1 <= row_num <= BOARD_SIZE):
        raise ValueError("Row must be between 1 and 8.")
    row = BOARD_SIZE - row_num
    return row, col
def parse_move_notation(move_text: str):
    """Parse move notation like b6-a5 into source and destination coordinates."""
    move_text = move_text.strip()
    if "-" not in move_text:
        raise ValueError("Move must use '-' notation, e.g., b6-a5.")
    src_text, dst_text = move_text.split("-", 1)
    src = position_to_coords(src_text)
    dst = position_to_coords(dst_text)
    return src, dst
class CheckersGame:
    """Implements standard 8x8 checkers rules, including forced captures and multi-capture continuation."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
    def _create_initial_board(self):
        """Create the standard starting board."""
        board = [[EMPTY for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)] for _ in range(BOARD_SIZE)]
        for row in range(3):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_2
        for row in range(5, 8):
            for col in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                if (row + col) % 2 == 1:
                    board[row][col] = PLAYER_1
        return board
    def reset(self):
        """Reset the game to the starting position."""
        self.board = self._create_initial_board()
        self.current_player = PLAYER_1
        self.game_over = False
        self.winner = None
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
    def piece_owner(self, piece: str):
        """Return the owner of a piece, or None for empty squares."""
        if piece in (PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING):
            return PLAYER_1
        if piece in (PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING):
            return PLAYER_2
        return None
    def is_king(self, piece: str):
        """Check whether a piece is a king."""
        return piece in (PLAYER_1_KING, PLAYER_2_KING)
    def _directions_for_piece(self, piece: str):
        """Get movement directions for a piece."""
        if piece == PLAYER_1:
            return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1)]
        if piece == PLAYER_2:
            return [(1, -1), (1, 1)]
        return [(-1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, -1), (1, 1)]
    def _enemy_pieces(self, player: str):
        """Return the set of enemy piece symbols for a player."""
        if player == PLAYER_1:
            return {PLAYER_2, PLAYER_2_KING}
        return {PLAYER_1, PLAYER_1_KING}
    def _in_bounds(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Check if a position is inside the board."""
        return 0 <= row < BOARD_SIZE and 0 <= col < BOARD_SIZE
    def get_valid_moves_for_piece(self, row: int, col: int):
        """
        Return valid moves for a piece.
        Result format:
        {
            "simple": [(r, c), ...],
            "captures": [((mid_r, mid_c), (dest_r, dest_c)), ...]
        }
        """
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner != self.current_player:
            return {"simple": [], "captures": []}
        simple_moves = []
        captures = []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            if self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] == EMPTY:
                    simple_moves.append((r1, c1))
                elif self.board[r1][c1] in enemies:
                    r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
                    if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                        captures.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return {"simple": simple_moves, "captures": captures}
    def _has_any_capture_for_player(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has at least one capture available."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def has_any_valid_move(self, player: str):
        """Determine if the given player has any legal move."""
        current = self.current_player
        self.current_player = player
        try:
            for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                    piece = self.board[r][c]
                    if self.piece_owner(piece) == player:
                        moves = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(r, c)
                        if moves["simple"] or moves["captures"]:
                            return True
            return False
        finally:
            self.current_player = current
    def _available_captures_from(self, row: int, col: int):
        """Return capture destinations for a specific piece, ignoring turn restrictions."""
        piece = self.board[row][col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return []
        owner = self.piece_owner(piece)
        if owner is None:
            return []
        dirs = self._directions_for_piece(piece)
        enemies = self._enemy_pieces(owner)
        caps = []
        for dr, dc in dirs:
            r1, c1 = row + dr, col + dc
            r2, c2 = row + 2 * dr, col + 2 * dc
            if self._in_bounds(r2, c2) and self._in_bounds(r1, c1):
                if self.board[r1][c1] in enemies and self.board[r2][c2] == EMPTY:
                    caps.append(((r1, c1), (r2, c2)))
        return caps
    def apply_move(self, src_row: int, src_col: int, dst_row: int, dst_col: int):
        """Apply a move if it is legal."""
        if self.game_over:
            return MoveResult(False, "Game is over. Please reset to play again.")
        if not self._in_bounds(src_row, src_col) or not self._in_bounds(dst_row, dst_col):
            return MoveResult(False, "Move is out of bounds.")
        if self.forced_capture_piece is not None and (src_row, src_col) != self.forced_capture_piece:
            return MoveResult(False, "You must continue capturing with the same piece.")
        piece = self.board[src_row][src_col]
        if piece == EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "No piece at source square.")
        if self.piece_owner(piece) != self.current_player:
            return MoveResult(False, "It is not that piece's turn.")
        if self.board[dst_row][dst_col] != EMPTY:
            return MoveResult(False, "Destination square is not empty.")
        legal = self.get_valid_moves_for_piece(src_row, src_col)
        capture_move = None
        if (dst_row, dst_col) in legal["simple"]:
            if self.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture chain is active and you must keep capturing.")
            if self._has_any_capture_for_player(self.current_player):
                return MoveResult(False, "A capture is available and must be taken.")
        else:
            for cap in legal["captures"]:
                if cap[1] == (dst_row, dst_col):
                    capture_move = cap
                    break
            if capture_move is None:
                return MoveResult(False, "Illegal move.")
        if capture_move:
            mid_r, mid_c = capture_move[0]
            self.board[mid_r][mid_c] = EMPTY
        self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = piece
        self.board[src_row][src_col] = EMPTY
        became_king = False
        if piece == PLAYER_1 and dst_row == 0:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_1_KING
            became_king = True
        elif piece == PLAYER_2 and dst_row == BOARD_SIZE - 1:
            self.board[dst_row][dst_col] = PLAYER_2_KING
            became_king = True
        if capture_move:
            continued_captures = self._available_captures_from(dst_row, dst_col)
            if continued_captures and not became_king:
                self.forced_capture_piece = (dst_row, dst_col)
                return MoveResult(True, "Capture applied successfully. Another capture is required from the same piece.")
        self.forced_capture_piece = None
        self.current_player = PLAYER_2 if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else PLAYER_1
        if not self.has_any_valid_move(self.current_player):
            self.game_over = True
            self.winner = PLAYER_1 if self.current_player == PLAYER_2 else PLAYER_2
        return MoveResult(True, "Move applied successfully.")
    def board_text(self):
        """Return a text representation of the board."""
        lines = []
        header = "   " + " ".join(list("abcdefgh"))
        lines.append(header)
        for r in range(BOARD_SIZE):
            row_num = BOARD_SIZE - r
            line = [f"{row_num}  "]
            for c in range(BOARD_SIZE):
                line.append(self.board[r][c])
            lines.append(" ".join(line))
        return "\n".join(lines)
    def get_winner(self):
        """Return the winner symbol if the game is over."""
        return self.winner
    def current_player_name(self):
        """Human-readable current player name."""
        return "Red" if self.current_player == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
```
gui.py
```python
'''
command-line interface for the checkers game application
'''
from game import CheckersGame, parse_move_notation, PLAYER_1, PLAYER_2, coords_to_position
class CheckersGUI:
    """Simple command-line interface for playing checkers."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.game = CheckersGame()
    def _player_name(self, symbol: str) -> str:
        """Convert player symbol to display name."""
        return "Red" if symbol == PLAYER_1 else "Black"
    def _print_help(self):
        """Print game instructions."""
        print("\nCommands:")
        print("  move like: b6-a5")
        print("  reset     : restart the game")
        print("  quit      : exit the game")
        print("")
    def _safe_input(self, prompt: str):
        """Read input safely and return None on EOF so tests/non-interactive runs do not crash."""
        try:
            return input(prompt)
        except EOFError:
            return None
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            return None
    def run(self):
        """Run the interactive game loop."""
        print("Checkers (Draughts)")
        print("Use move notation like b6-a5")
        self._print_help()
        while True:
            print(self.game.board_text())
            if self.game.game_over:
                winner = self.game.get_winner()
                if winner:
                    print(f"\nGame over! Winner: {self._player_name(winner)}")
                else:
                    print("\nGame over!")
                break
            print(f"\n{self.game.current_player_name()}'s turn")
            if self.game.forced_capture_piece is not None:
                forced_row, forced_col = self.game.forced_capture_piece
                print(f"Forced capture continuation from {coords_to_position(forced_row, forced_col)}")
            move_raw = self._safe_input("Enter move (or 'help', 'reset', 'quit'): ")
            if move_raw is None:
                print("\nNo input available. Exiting cleanly.")
                break
            move_text = move_raw.strip()
            if not move_text:
                print("Please enter a move.")
                continue
            lowered = move_text.lower()
            if lowered == "quit":
                print("Goodbye.")
                break
            if lowered == "help":
                self._print_help()
                continue
            if lowered == "reset":
                self.game.reset()
                print("Game reset.")
                continue
            try:
                (src_row, src_col), (dst_row, dst_col) = parse_move_notation(move_text)
                result = self.game.apply_move(src_row, src_col, dst_row, dst_col)
                print(result.message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
